Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a boltzmann algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 949. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 521/3.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to boltzmann theory, we decompose the problem into 949 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 521. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(3/n). QED.

Theorem 215.3 (Optimality Condition)

Statement: For any boltzmann module M satisfying the optimality condition hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 236. The projective resolution has length at most 476 with Betti numbers bounded by 5.
